// === Renames added 2026-05-11 (RogueSquadron64Recomp RE session) ===
// These names were applied to the splat ELF via llvm-objcopy --redefine-sym.
// Adding them here makes them durable across splat rebuilds.
// See /Projects/RogueSquadron64Recomp memory/project_recomp_function_map.md
// for the full subsystem documentation.

// Bootstrap / main game loop
// returns ptr to gBootConfig
getGameConfig       = 0x80000C44;
// boot thread; reads gBootConfig, calls loadDebugAssets/initSiQueue/setDisplayMode
mainBootstrapWorker = 0x80000C68;
// outer game loop, entry from gBootConfig+0x04
mainGameLoop        = 0x8003DFA0;

// Service-worker subsystem (generic message-queue worker pattern)
// zeros service registry head/tail/count at 0x801128D0
initServiceRegistry        = 0x80006C00;
// (numMsgSlots, stackSize, threadPriority, entryFn) -> uniqueId
registerServiceWorker      = 0x80006C28;
// looks up worker by id, osStartThread
startServiceWorker         = 0x80006D9C;
// SAVE/LOAD message state machine (msg=1=SAVE uses adler32+EEPROM write, msg=2=LOAD)
saveLoadDispatcher         = 0x80006798;
// walks registry by id, osRecvMesg(BLOCK)
recvServiceMessage         = 0x80006F24;
// non-blocking variant of recvServiceMessage
tryRecvServiceMessage      = 0x80006EC4;
// producer; walks registry by id, osSendMesg
sendServiceMessage         = 0x80006F78;
// (key) → osRecvMesg(NOBLOCK), if got msg, osJamMesg restores it to queue front; returns 1 if msg pending
peekServiceMessage         = 0x80006E48;
// (key, msg) → osSendMesg(node+0x1D0, msg, BLOCK) — direct post to worker's queue
postMessageToServiceWorker = 0x80007070;
// (key) → osDestroyThread, state=0, unlinks node from registry, rs_free
unregisterServiceWorker    = 0x800070BC;
// 6 callers (mainEventQueueWorker, piDmaWorker, pollDmaSlotStep, setupAssetDma...): osYieldThread + return 1
yieldThreadRet1            = 0x800072AC;
// (key, prio) → walks registry, sets node[+0x4]=prio, osSetThreadPri(node+0x20, prio)
setServiceWorkerPriority   = 0x8000725C;
// returns MEM_BU(dmaSlotMutex) — single-instruction byte getter for the DMA-slot mutex flag
getDmaSlotMutex            = 0x8000533C;
// (prio) — clamps to [1, 0x20], looks up the DMA service-worker ID stored at 0x80110D54, calls setServiceWorkerPriority(id, prio_byte)
setDmaWorkerPriority       = 0x8000534C;
// reads ptr from D_80112724 (save data buffer, malloc'd by initSaveData), calls rs_free
freeSaveDataBuffer         = 0x80006764;
// (callback) — stores callback fn ptr at D_801126F0 (s32 (*)(?) per docs/save_data/func_80006338.c); used by initSaveData to register save-complete callback
setSaveDataCallback        = 0x8000678C;
// (key) → walks registry, sets state=4, osStopThread(node+0x20)
stopServiceWorker          = 0x800072CC;

// Audio mixer S16 sample helpers (tail-call chain into companding saturator)
// weighted mix 0.25*t2 + 0.75*t4, falls through to audioCompandS16
audioMixSampleS16_1_3 = 0x8009DD3C;
// equal mix 0.5*t2 + 0.5*t4, falls through to audioCompandS16
audioMixSampleS16_1_1 = 0x8009DD54;
// weighted mix 0.75*t2 + 0.25*t4, falls through to audioCompandS16
audioMixSampleS16_3_1 = 0x8009DD64;
// 3-region piecewise-linear soft-clip on $t2: |x|<0x2B33→2x, <0x4E66→x+0x2B33, else→x/4+0x6600; clamp ±0x7FFF; restore sign
audioCompandS16       = 0x8009DD7C;
// mixes 8 s16 sample pairs from voice buffer (L at +0x168, R at +0x178) via $t3 mix-function pointer; packs results to t6..t9
audioMixStereoBlock8  = 0x8009DC98;
// MusyX-style audio init; references "N64 RSP mixer V1.0" string; clamps voiceCount to 0x20, installs DMEM-mixer callback function pointers at 0x801499D0..0x801499E0
initN64RspMixer       = 0x8009189C;

// Save / account subsystem
// save-service worker thread (key=auto, slots=4)
saveServiceWorker         = 0x8006F2CC;
// calls registerServiceWorker for saveServiceWorker
registerSaveService       = 0x8006EF98;
// allocates 20B msg, sends to saveServiceWorker
triggerSaveMessage        = 0x8006F274;
// copies game-state bytes from 0x80130B47 to working buffer at 0x80145AB0
loadAccountDataIntoStruct = 0x8006EAAC;
// post-level save init (medal screen path)
postLevelSaveInit         = 0x800C06D0;

// NPC update dispatcher (gNpcSlotList: 0x800-entry list, gNpcContextArray: 0xC0 NPC contexts)
// Per docs/npc_stuff/npc_stuff.md - each NPC context has update_func ptr at +0x00
// walks gNpcSlotList, calls each NPC update_func per frame
slotDispatcherIter        = 0x8003E8DC;
// inner per-slot dispatch; mallocs D_80130BC0/BC4 linked list entries
slotDispatcherInner       = 0x8003EA4C;
// dispatches effect handlers from cinematic loop
slotEffectHandlerDispatch = 0x800A71B8;

// Cinematic timing chain
// inner cinematic frame loop
cinematicLoopBody       = 0x800A5D80;
// sets state ptr at 0x800B0934
cinematicInitializer    = 0x800AF408;
// per-frame stage counter advance
cinematicStageAdvancer  = 0x800AF550;
// clears cinematic state
cinematicDeactivator    = 0x800AF60C;
// per-frame delta-time computer
cinematicComputeDt      = 0x800AF360;
// computes interpolation ratio from byte counter / float threshold
cinematicInterpRatio    = 0x800AF668;
// returns 1 if MEM_W(0x800B0934) != 0 (cinematic state ptr set by cinematicInitializer), else 0
isCinematicActive       = 0x800AF540;

// SI (Serial Interface / controllers)
// controller polling thread
siServiceThread   = 0x80002E10;
// initializes SI message queue + flags (0x80110740-748)
initSiQueue       = 0x80003308;
// spin-yields until 0x80110741 (SI busy flag) clears
waitForSerialIdle = 0x80003284;
// sibling of waitForSerialIdle - releases the SI lock
releaseSerialLock = 0x800032E0;

// Save subsystem (CONFIRMED via docs/save_data/save_data.md)
// EEPROM sanity check + reinit on mismatch
initSaveData          = 0x80006338;
// loads 10 default high scores from D_8003CB10
loadDefaultHighScores = 0x8006DCCC;
// bubble sort for high score list
highScoreBubbleSort   = 0x8006DD50;

// Heap allocator helpers
// heap free-list walker (used by rs_malloc/rs_free chain)
heapWalker          = 0x800079F0;
// dequeue from heap free-list head
heapFreeListDequeue = 0x8000CFD4;

// DMA / asset loader subsystem (CONFIRMED via docs/dma_slots/dma_slots.md)
// initializes 8 DMA slot state + msg queues
initDmaSlots            = 0x800045E8;
// submits a DMA via osPiStartDma; round-robin slot allocator
submitDmaSlot           = 0x80003480;
// walks 32B manifest entries at 0x80110A74 by name string
findManifestEntryByName = 0x800047F4;
// loads "dbg_data"/"dbg_font" assets at boot
loadDebugAssets         = 0x800246E8;
// producer for 8-slot x 116B ring at 0x80111D60 (likely DMA-related)
pushEventToRingBuffer   = 0x80003BCC;
// claims slot in 0x80111BC0 table (likely DMA-related)
subscribeEventHandler   = 0x80003C98;

// Event/object workers
// worker that drains service msgs into ring buffer
mainEventQueueWorker         = 0x80004360;
// cartridge PI DMA worker; ring at 0x801112B0 (256 x 20B); osPiStartDma
piDmaWorker                  = 0x80005570;
// creates message queue at 0x80112BA0, clears producer/consumer indices, registers piDmaWorker as service worker (callback=0x80005570, prio=4, qsz=0x200), stores handle at 0x801126BC
initPiDmaWorker              = 0x80005850;
// checks ring producer/consumer at 0x801126B0/B4; if mismatch and busy flag set, sends service message to piDmaWorker queue at 0x801126BC. Returns 1=dispatched, 0=idle. Polled in yield loops by submitDmaSlot/waitDmaSlotComplete.
dispatchPendingPiDmaToWorker = 0x800058C0;
// MISNAMED-IN-RECOMP. Not a service worker — per-frame texture-material-expiry walker called from frameStartReset (funcs_3.c:8096). Reads count at MEM_HU[0x80128EF0]; if 0, early-exits. Walks materialListHead[] at MEM_W[0x80128F00] (same head used by unlinkTextureMaterialList at 0x80021F78). Per entry: bumps +0x8 age halfword, when over threshold MEM_BU[0x80128EAD] calls func_800079A4(entry+0x14) (cleanup), unlinks via +0x0/+0x4 prev-next, splices into free-list at MEM_W[0x80128EF8]. Stubbed in toml as "unidentifiedServiceWorker" — early-exit defended against AV that's already handled by the zero-count gate. Re-enabling should be safe but the stub is redundant when count is properly inited.
tickTextureMaterialExpiry    = 0x80022048;
// level-time HUD worker; processes material/texture update msgs (per docs/hud_stuff/hud_stuff.md)
hudDisplayUpdateWorker       = 0x800495FC;
// material_id -> D_8011A444 -> D_80128F08 -> texture_data ptr
getTextureDataByMaterialId   = 0x800232F8;

// Display mode
// validates mode < 0x21, stores at MEM_B[0x80141AD0]; calls initAudioSubsystem (full audio init incl thread/MQ/event creation) + func_80097120 + func_80097088 + func_8008E614 + func_80097D60 + func_80099C2C (state-reset chain). Boot-time variant.
setDisplayMode     = 0x8008EA14;
// runtime mode-change variant of setDisplayMode. Same mode validation + same state-reset chain (func_80097120/088/D60/099C2C + func_8008E614), but calls reapplyAudioConfig (mixer + freq + mutex, no thread creation) instead of full initAudioSubsystem. 1 caller in funcs_0.c (display-mode-change runtime path).
reapplyDisplayMode = 0x8008EB5C;
// reconfigures audio without thread creation: initFactor5Mutex + osAiSetFrequency + initN64RspMixer. Companion to initAudioSubsystem (which adds the thread creation). 1 caller: reapplyDisplayMode.
reapplyAudioConfig = 0x80091C88;
// menu overlay init (calls registerSaveService)
menuOverlayInit    = 0x800C58A0;

// Misc utility
// fills time-snapshot struct
timeSnapshotFiller = 0x8000BC00;
// f0 = f12 - trunc(f12/f14)*f14, then *f14
floatModulo        = 0x8001E20C;

// Factor 5 recursive mutex primitives (832A0.s) - STUBBED in recomp
// creates queue D_80149990 + posts initial token
initFactor5Mutex          = 0x80091FC4;
// recursive: if D_800A1780 > 0 increment; else osRecvMesg
factor5MutexAcquire       = 0x80092010;
// decrement D_800A1780; if reaches 0, osSendMesg
factor5MutexRelease       = 0x8009205C;
// low-level osRecvMesg(BLOCK), no counter touch
factor5QueueBlock         = 0x800920A4;
// low-level osSendMesg(NOBLOCK), no counter touch
factor5QueueSignal        = 0x800920D0;
// busy-spins on D_8014998A halfword (hangs without MusyX RSP task)
waitForMusyXAudioTaskDone = 0x80091EB0;

// MusyX synth voice pipeline (RE'd this session via the Python SoundMacro VM in tools/musyx_*.py).
// Operates on the RSP-mixer voice-slot array installed by initN64RspMixer: base ptr 0x801496F8, stride 0x88.
// per-frame synth tick: tickAudioSequencerFrame then build cmd-list + mix per output block
musyxSynthFrame            = 0x8009123C;
// per voice: keyOn if +0x22==0 else append to RSP cmd-list (head 0x80149750, count 0x80149760)
musyxBuildVoiceCommandList = 0x80091034;
// iterates voice array, calls musyxRenderVoiceSamples per active voice into the frame block
musyxMixActiveVoices       = 0x80090E04;
// per-voice resample/mix kernel: steps +0x4 rate / +0x8 pos through loop +0x3C/+0x40 into out buffer
musyxRenderVoiceSamples    = 0x8008FFAC;

musyxKeyOnVoice            = 0x80090A3C;
// (voiceIdx, sampleDesc, adsrFlag): zero slot, load loop/base/sampPtr from descriptor, default pitch +0x4=0x1000
musyxInitVoiceFromSample   = 0x80092128;

musyxSetVoiceAdsrFromTable = 0x8008A8C4;
// writes attack+0x28 decay+0x2A sustain(float)+0x2C release+0x30 into the 0x88 voice slot
musyxStoreVoiceAdsrParams  = 0x800922C4;
// SoundMacro key-off: state +0x6C=3, capture env level +0x5C->+0x68, enter ADSR release
musyxStartVoiceRelease     = 0x80092274;
// key-on trigger: +0x0=1 active, zero env accumulators, +0x64=1.0f, +0x28->+0x58
musyxTriggerVoiceAttack    = 0x8009231C;

musyxStopVoice             = 0x80092368;
// (voiceIdx, value): clamp <=0x2000, store into slot +0x4 pitch/period field
musyxSetVoicePitchBend     = 0x80092390;

// MusyX song-playback path (RE'd 2026-06-01). Gate = gMusyXActiveFlag 0x800A0F50; song-slot table
// base 0x80149A00 stride 0xEF8 (8 slots), same table tickMusyXIfActive ticks. See startSongSequence above.
// gated play wrapper: if gMusyXActiveFlag==0 return (no music); else factor5MutexAcquire -> processSongByHandle(a0,a1,0) -> release. Callers: updateHangarBayScene, func_800ED200
playSongIfMusyXActive = 0x80096C88;
// resolves an active song by handle (struct+0x0 & 0x7FFFFFFF) in the 0xEF8 slot table; sgid=struct+0x14 sid=struct+0x16; dispatches per-track/region handlers. Callers: func_80094958, playSongIfMusyXActive
processSongByHandle   = 0x800966A4;
// predicate: if gMusyXActiveFlag set, return (HU[a0+0x8] & 0x1); leaf off the spatial-SFX side (sibling of play3DSoundEvent), NOT a voice allocator
isAudioVoiceActive    = 0x800989B8;
// hangar bay camera/scene update (gated hangarCameraState 0x800CC3E4==2, indexes per-bay tables by gHangarCurrentBay 0x800CD6E8); reached via LOOKUP_FUNC from func_800AAB70/func_800AAF90; calls playSongIfMusyXActive once (the bay music)
updateHangarBayScene  = 0x800AB658;

// Song-command dispatch handlers — processSongByHandle fans out to these per-slot appliers (slot table
// base 0x80149A00 stride 0xEF8; control block slot+0xEC0..0xEF0, cmd-flag byte slot+0xEEE). 2026-06-01.
// public "play music by id": resolve (sgid,sid) in song-bank table 0x801529D0 (count 0x80152A10, sub-entry stride 0x84) -> factor5 mutex -> startSongSequence. ~30 callers across gameplay overlays
playSongById             = 0x80097C2C;
// same (sgid,sid)->startSongSequence, but a control byte selects already-locked vs mutex-wrap path; only caller = processSongByHandle (looped/re-issue path). Has the [songplay] log
playSongByIdLockCtl      = 0x80097AD0;
// locked inner body of queueAudioVoiceCmd 0x800964C8: find song slot, a3==0 iterates the 0x40-entry voice table at slot+0x528 emitting note-on via func_8008DD60; a3>=1 writes control fields/flags
queueAudioVoiceCmdLocked = 0x80096284;
// song-cmd applier (cmd-flag 0x20): latch u16 arg into slot+0xEC2 and slot+0xEEC, set flag bit 0x20 at slot+0xEEE. (mechanism verified; exact musical role — transpose? — unconfirmed)
applySongU16ParamCmd     = 0x80095EA8;
// song-cmd applier: set state byte slot+0xEC0=1, clear flag bit 0x08 at slot+0xEEE; called first/unconditionally to quiesce the resolved sub-song before re-issuing commands
quiesceSongSlotCmd       = 0x80095FEC;
// song-cmd applier (cmd-flag 0x10): store two u32 args into slot+0x110/+0x114 (live) and slot+0xEE4/+0xEE8 (latched), OR flag bit 0x10. (pan/volume-ramp class — exact role unconfirmed)
setSongParamPairCmd      = 0x80096118;

// Per-frame song sequence stepper (drives the handlers above). tickMusyXIfActive -> tickActiveSongs.
// iterates the 8 song slots (base 0x80149A00 stride 0xEF8), calls tickSongSequence per active slot
tickActiveSongs   = 0x80095DA0;
// per-song tick: advances the sequence (via advanceSongRegion) and dispatches song commands (processSongByHandle/playSongById/quiesceSongSlotCmd/queueAudioVoiceCmdLocked/applySongU16ParamCmd); recursive for sub-songs
tickSongSequence  = 0x80095ADC;
// walks/advances the song's region+event cursor (slot+0x12C/+0x134/+0x120), reads next event, checks the 0xFFFF event-stream terminator, resets cursor on wrap
advanceSongRegion = 0x80095244;

// Mission/DAT/HUD/Account renames (batches 18-22, 2026-05-11)

// loads a texture into D_80128F08 chain
loadTextureToMemory             = 0x8001F954;
// canonical tile loader: emits the texture-load GBI chain (G_SETTIMG 0xFD/G_LOADTLUT 0xF0/G_SETTILE 0xF5/G_LOADBLOCK 0xF3/G_SETTILESIZE + load/pipe syncs) into the DL buffer. Used by the per-glyph text path (caller func_80021A1C)
loadTextureTile                 = 0x8001FE74;
// 6-case jump table mapping texture-format codes 0..5 to RDP G_IM_FMT_* values, preserves upper tile-mode flag bits (0x80-0x8000). Called from texture-descriptor parsers.
decodeRdpFormatFlags            = 0x8001E978;
// sister to decodeRdpFormatFlags; 6-case jump table mapping texture-size codes (mask 0xF) to RDP G_IM_SIZ_* values, preserves upper bits. Called from loadTextureToMemory.
decodeRdpSizeFlags              = 0x8001EA50;
// takes (texIdx); first calls unlinkTextureMaterialList to dispose materials, then locates slot in 36-byte-stride texture LUT at 0x80128F08[texIdx], computes (base+size) tail-extent vs heap top (0x800FA7D8), and shifts trailing texture data down by entry.size to compact. Called from releaseTextureLutEntry.
freeTextureDataSlot             = 0x8001E7D0;
// (texIdx u16, format u8) — releases TLUT/palette block for a texture: CI4 format (1) uses 16-color (32-byte) stride, else 256-color (512-byte) stride. Reads ptr from texture LUT (0x80128EFC) by texIdx*4, shifts subsequent TLUT memory at MEM[0x8011A7D0] down to compact. Called from freeTextureDataSlot.
freeTextureTlutBlock            = 0x8001E6E0;
// (texIdx u16, tlutSrc u32, format u32, count u16, ...) — allocates a TLUT/palette block in the texture LUT: format & 0xF selects size (0 → 16-color/32B, 1 → 256-color/512B, else returns 0xFFFF). If texIdx == 0xFFFF, scans texture LUT at 0x80128EFC for a free slot. Validates that tlutSrc is a kseg0 (0x80000000-prefix) ptr. Companion allocator to freeTextureTlutBlock; calls the per-format pixel-pack helper packTlutEntriesRgba5551.
allocTextureTlutBlock           = 0x8001E5A0;
// (dst, formatFlag, src, count, [sp+0x10]=alphaSrc) — packs `count` 4-byte source palette entries into RGBA5551 halfwords at `dst`. Source byte layout reads bytes at src+1 (R), src+0 (B), src-1 (G); alpha bit derived per-pixel from alphaSrc byte 0 (set if non-zero, else 0). Result halfword = (R<<11 & 0xF800) | (G<<3 & 0x7C0) | (B>>2 & 0x3E) | A. Called from allocTextureTlutBlock.
packTlutEntriesRgba5551         = 0x8001E36C;
// (name str) — searches the loaded-texture name table: count at MEM_HU[0x8011A8B4], indices at 0x8011A444 (stride 2 halfwords). For each entry, switches between primary (texture LUT at 0x80128F08, 36-byte stride, name at +0x14) and alternate (MEM_W[0x801143AC] table, 16-byte entries, gated by halfword bit 0x2) source based on flags. Returns matching index via rs_strncmp(name, table_entry, 0x10), or 0 if not found.
findTextureByName               = 0x80022D68;
// (texLutEntry, bufPtrPtr, ?, ?) — main texture decode driver: if *bufPtrPtr is null, rs_mallocs a buffer (size from texLutEntry+0xC), stores at *bufPtrPtr, marks owner flag at texLutEntry+0x2. Looks up real texture index via gTextureIndex (0x8011A444), reads w/h/format/etc from main texture LUT (0x80128F08, 36-byte stride), calls parseImageFile to decode the asset stream, then applies tile-mode flags (bit 0x800 / 0x400). Called from func_800547C0.
loadAndDecodeTexture            = 0x80022FF8;
// (key u32, fmt u8, alphaFlag u8, hasAlphaSrc u8) — TLUT pack wrapper that always searches for a free slot then allocates fresh storage. Builds flag word (0x10-XOR'd fmt bit | 0x8000 if alphaFlag | 0x4000 if hasAlphaSrc), validates key has 0x8000_xxxx magic, walks TLUT registry at 0x80128EFC[count=0x80128F04] for value==0, decrements high-water allocator at 0x8012A7D0 by sizeof(entries)*2, writes new chunk into slot, then calls packTlutEntriesRgba5551. Format & 0xF dispatches size: 0 → 16 entries / 1 → 256 entries / else returns 0xFFFF.
texPackTlutAtAllocSlot          = 0x800233B0;
// (slotHint u16, key u32, fmt u8, alphaFlag u8, hasAlphaSrc u8 [sp+0x30]) — sister of texPackTlutAtAllocSlot that accepts a hint slot index. If slotHint==0xFFFF behaves like the search-and-alloc path; otherwise reuses MEM[0x80128EFC + slotHint*4] as the destination chunk and calls packTlutEntriesRgba5551 directly. Same fmt & 0xF size dispatch (16 vs 256 entries).
texPackTlutSlotOrAlloc          = 0x80023514;
// (slotIdx u16, basePtr, offsetMul u8, fmtCount u8, byteMode u8 [sp+0x33], hasAlpha u8 [sp+0x34]) — direct TLUT dispatcher with no slot mgmt or magic-key check: reads MEM[0x80128EFC + slotIdx*4], adds (basePtr + offsetMul*4) when byteMode is set or (basePtr + offsetMul*3) otherwise, then forwards to packTlutEntriesRgba5551. Hot-path entry used when caller has already resolved the slot.
texPackTlutAtIndex              = 0x80023678;
// (val u8) — one-line setter for the profiler-bar debug flag at MEM_B[0x80038CE0]. Read by emitProfilerBarSegment as its early-out gate. The retail ROM ships with the flag at 0 (HUD dormant) but the codepath is intact.
setProfilerBarGate              = 0x80023D30;
// (dlBuf, microseconds, rowY i16, alpha u8, [sp+0x10]=fillByte u8, [sp+0x14]=zero) — emits one horizontal time-bar into the active display-list buffer as chained RDP commands (0xB500 LOADSYNC, 0xE700 FULLSYNC, 0xB900 SETOTHERMODE_L, 0xF0A4000 SETCONVERT, then TEXRECTs). Converts the µs value via (val*1e9 / MEM_W[0x80039100..04]) / 0x32DCD5, clamps pixel width to 0x140 (320 = NTSC visible). Allocates more DL buffer via allocateDisplayListBuffer when current chunk overflows. Returns updated DL ptr in v0. Gated by debug flag MEM_BU[0x80038CE0] — early-out as no-op when 0. Called 7× from func_8000B6F4 to draw a row-stacked profiler bar overlay.
emitProfilerBarSegment          = 0x80023D40;
// per-frame display-list chunk allocator: pops a 0x100-byte buffer from freelist head at MEM_W[0x801173B0] (refills via findAndUnlinkSmallestEntry → walk-and-append if empty). Sets globals: cursor at 0x8012DC58 = chunk+0x8, end-of-chunk at 0x801183D4 = chunk+0x100, active-buf at 0x80124C0 = chunk. Chains via G_NOOP (0x70000000) into prior DL's tail (ptr at 0x8012A4C0), and writes G_DL (0x60000000, 0x80037658) at chunk+0x8 — the static-DL header. Then iterates count at MEM_W[0x80117C00] emitting per-slot LOADSYNC+G_DL preamble jumps from a halfword index table at 0x80124470. Called from initRenderStateArrays (main frame) and func_8000B6F4 (profiler overlay).
beginFrameDLChunk               = 0x8000A8A0;
// (cueType u8, halfword_id u16, dataPtr, positionPtr (XYZ floats), [sp+0x58]=auxId u32, [sp+0x5C]=auxFloat) — primary cue/event submitter (28 callers across game). 13-case switch on (cueType - 2) at L_8005E448 selects per-category handler; pulls a cue ID halfword from a per-player localized table at MEM_W[0x80137CE4] + (playerIdx × stride) + MEM_W[0x80129610] using category-specific offset (+0x0, +0x10, +0x20, +0x38). Builds local cue descriptor at sp+0x1C..0x30 (slot, position × global scale at MEM_W[0x80043120], cue ID). Re-dispatches through a 14-case switch on (cueType - 1) at L_8005E6D8. Gates noopHandler calls and increments counter at MEM_W[0x80130B7C] only when mute byte at MEM_BU[0x80130B39] is 0. Listener position via getPlayerField2(0).
dispatchCueByCategory           = 0x8005E3B0;
// (state, mode u8 [0/1/else], altState, outFloatPtr) — ticks one of 3 (raw_timer i16, norm_float, advanced_flag u8) triplets. Mode 0 → state+0x15C/+0x158/+0x161. Mode 1 → state+0x188/+0x184/+0x18D. Else → altState+0x24/+0x20/+0x29. Reads the raw timer as i16, normalizes via division by float at MEM_W[0x800A89D4] (max-range constant), then calls sister func_800F1310 (~heavy actor-state updater that transforms position and resolves NPC slot via findActiveNpcInSlotChain). Writes the normalized value back to the matching float slot; sets the "advanced" byte flag to 1 if the value changed, else 0. When `outFloatPtr` (a3) is non-null, also publishes the new normalized value (or 0 if unchanged) there. 45 callers across funcs_36..40 — primary entry for ticking per-actor animation/interp channels.
tickProgressChannel             = 0x800F1870;
// (outMatrix=a0, ?=a1, f20=a2 float, randomSpread i32=a3, [sp+0x70]=fixedYaw i32, [sp+0x74]=fixedPitch i32) — builds a 3D rotation matrix at outMatrix typically sp+0x10. Picks random roll in [0, randomSpread) via rand_recomp() % a3 (returns 0 if spread==0), passes to buildRotationMatrixSlot0. Picks a second random angle (raw int16) via rand_recomp(), composes via composeRotationMatrixSlot1. Then optionally composes with two fixed angles from stack: fixedYaw via composeRotationMatrixSlot0 (skipped when 0), and fixedPitch via similar (truncated). 35 callers across game — the standard "spawn-direction randomizer" for particles/debris/projectile cones.
composeRandomRotationMatrix     = 0x8007202C;
// (playerSlot u16, channelIdx u16, phase f32, ...) — samples a keyframed animation channel from a player slot. a0 indexes gPlayerSlotTable (0x8009FC10) to fetch player ptr. a1 indexes into the channel sub-table at player+0x34 (count u16 at +0x8, key array halfwords at +0xC, value array halfwords at +0x10). Walks all entries finding the bounding (min,max) value pair, computes interpolated result scaled by globals at MEM_W[0x8003B73C..0x8003B740]. Calls func_80063A68 per-iteration (probably the keyframe-fetch helper). Combines with player base offset at player+0x6. 29 callers — primary entry for player-anim sampling during cinematic / scripted scenes.
samplePlayerAnimChannel         = 0x80061F34;
// (cueType u8) — 1-line wrapper that forwards a0 to initSoundCueRequest at 0x800EFCD4. 28 callers across game (gameplay events, weapon fire, UI clicks, etc). Companion wrappers at 0x800C7D60 → func_800EF770 (named-asset trigger) and 0x800C7D7C → func_800EF95C are sister entry points for different cue subsystems.
triggerSoundCueByType           = 0x800C7D98;
// (cueType u8 in [0..7]) — initializes the pending audio cue config struct at MEM_W[0x8010C200]: byte +0x8 = 0x7F (volume 127/127), halfword +0x4 = (cueType==3 ? 0x1D : 0x1E) priority, halfword +0x6 = MEM_HU[0x8010B304 + cueType*2] (the actual sound asset ID), halfword +0xA = 1 (request flag), halfwords +0x10..+0x12 = 0x64 (pan center), byte +0xF = 0, halfword +0xC..0xE bytes = 0. Also raises two pending-request flags: MEM_B[0x8010C262] = 1, MEM_B[0x8010C268] = 1. Early-out (no change) when cueType >= 8.
initSoundCueRequest             = 0x800EFCD4;
// (state, dispatchArg, dataPtr, sharedPtr, [f24], [f25], [sp+0x7B]=byte, [sp+0x7C]=...) — top-level "fire effect" entry, 54 callers across funcs_19/36..40. Reads state+0x14C float via getEffectIntensity (returned in f0). Submits a cue via dispatchCueByCategory with the asset's halfword ID from state+0x198, the f0 intensity, and the caller's float/byte aux params. If the caller's f22 float is strictly > 0.0, additionally calls slotDispatcherIter(returnedSlot, 0xA, &localTbl) to fire the slot-A effect chain. Used as the "play SFX + spawn visual" composite trigger.
triggerCueWithSlotEffect        = 0x800F2FCC;
// (state) -> f0 — 2-instr leaf getter: returns f0 = MEM_W[state+0x14C] (the per-asset effect intensity float). Companion to setEffectIntensity. Used by triggerCueWithSlotEffect to read intensity before submitting a cue.
getEffectIntensity              = 0x800F2FB4;
// (state, intensity f32) -> v0=1 — 2-instr leaf setter: writes MEM_W[state+0x14C] = a1 and returns 1 (always success). Companion to getEffectIntensity.
setEffectIntensity              = 0x800F2FC0;
// (state, positionPtr, scaleA f32, scaleB f32) — 22 callers, computes a world-space position anchored to an NPC. Reads state+0x130 (npc handle u16), resolves the active NPC via findActiveNpcInSlotChain + getNpcContextByIndex to get an NPC context (ctx). If ctx+0xC (mesh-part ref) is non-null, zmemcpy's a 0x30-byte transform from ctx+0x8 into local stack, then calls transformMeshPartIntoWorld(ctx+0xC, localTransform) to place the mesh part in world space. Otherwise uses state+0x8 as the base position. Calls func_800F2AC4(state, localTransform, scaleA, sp+0x40) — final transform helper — which writes 3 floats to sp+0x40..0x48 (final world position). Returns the deltas (sp+0x40 - positionPtr[0..8]) at sp+0x10..0x18 (and stores f0 at sp+0x50). Used by effect spawners to anchor visuals to an NPC's mesh part.
resolveNpcAttachmentPos         = 0x800F0F94;
// (actor, intensity f32) — 23 callers, type-keyed effect dispatcher on actor type byte at actor+0x112. Type 0 → no-op (early return). Type 1 → func_800F410C(actor, intensity). Type 2 → func_800F4E74. Type 3 → func_800F538C. Type 4 → func_800F8398. All handlers take (actor, intensity) as (a0, f0). Sister functions like func_800F9AB8 use the same +0x112 type byte for related per-type operations — actor+0x100..0x118 is the per-actor effect data block (+0x104..0x106 bytes, +0x108 word, +0x10C float intensity, +0x110 flags halfword, +0x112 type, +0x113..). Read state+0x110 flags via updateObjectFlags110.
dispatchEffectByActorType       = 0x800F9A14;
// (actor, angle1 f32, magnitude1 f32, angle2 f32, [sp+0x50]=magnitude2 f32) — 19 callers, initializes actor's motion/velocity parameter block at actor+0x114..+0x137 from 2 (angle, magnitude) pairs. Multiplies each angle by deg-to-rad scale at MEM_W[0x800A89EC] then passes to cosf_recomp. Multiplies each magnitude by scale at MEM_W[0x800A89F0]. Writes (cos1, mag1*scale, cos2, mag2*scale) into actor+0x114/+0x118/+0x11C/+0x120. Adds random ±8-step jitter (rand_recomp % 17) scaled by MEM_W[0x800A89F4] at actor+0x124. Resets state bytes: actor+0x135 = 1 (motion-active flag), +0x136 = 0, +0x137 = 0. Used by effect spawners to initialize particle/projectile motion from polar inputs.
initActorMotionParams           = 0x800F2888;
// (actor, srcXform, scale f32, mode, [sp+0x68]=outFloatPtr) — 11 callers, Euler integration wrapper around tickProgressChannel. zmemcpy's a 0x30-byte transform from a1 into local at sp+0x10. Reads velocity at local+0xC/+0x10/+0x14, integrates: local+0x0..+0x8 (position) += velocity × scale. Then forwards (actor, mode, local, outFloatPtr) to tickProgressChannel — when mode != 0/1, the function reads timer/norm/flag from this integrated local-state's +0x24/+0x20/+0x29 fields. Returns the tick result byte from tickProgressChannel.
stepAndTickProgressChannel      = 0x800F2F10;
// (actor, intensity f32, byte0 u8, byte1 u8, [sp+0x10]=byte2 u8, [sp+0x14]=param u32) -> v0 — 12 callers, type-guarded setter for the type-1 actor extended params block. Reads actor+0x112; if != 1, returns 0 immediately (type mismatch). On match (type == 1): writes intensity to actor+0x10C, byte0/1/2 to actor+0x104/+0x105/+0x106, param to actor+0x108. Returns 1 on success. Sister to dispatchEffectByActorType — operates on the same +0x112 type byte but is the WRITER for the type-1-specific +0x104..+0x10C block (vs. the dispatcher which is the type-keyed READER/INVOKER).
setType1ActorParams             = 0x800F9AB8;
// (actor, target f32, floor f32, duration f32, [sp+0x13]=channel u8) — 7 callers, type-1-guarded animation ramp updater. Returns immediately if actor+0x112 != 1. Selects between two parallel ramp channels at actor+0x48..+0x50 vs actor+0x50..+0x58 based on the channel byte (channel == 1 → actor+0x48 base, else → actor+0x50 base via a0 += 8). Per-channel layout (currentValue, delta, targetValue): writes scaled target, clamps to floor, recomputes delta. When delta > 0 also writes target to channel+0x10 and 1/duration to channel+0x14 (the inverse-duration speed). Otherwise rolls current = target and rescales actor+0x2C by MEM_W[actor+0x24]+0x24.
updateType1AnimRamp             = 0x800F9B34;
// (actor, intensity f32) — type-1 actor effect executor, dispatched from dispatchEffectByActorType when actor+0x112 == 1. ~600-instr handler (stack 0x1A8). Reads actor+0x198 (cue id) → getNpcContextField8 to get NPC ref. Reads actor+0xD8/+0xDC/+0xE0 (a base position triplet) and stages it on the stack. Reads s5+0x104 = actor+0x10C (type-1 intensity from setType1ActorParams) and gates further work on whether it's non-zero. The remainder is type-1 specific effect rendering / state update.
executeActorType1Effect         = 0x800F410C;
// (actor, intensity f32) — type-2 effect executor, dispatched from dispatchEffectByActorType. Stack 0x88. Same prologue as type-1 (reads actor+0x198 → getNpcContextField8 → NPC ctx in s3). Reads ctx+0x24 (likely a scale/distance ref) and ctx+0x0 (position). Diverges from type-1 with shorter / position-update-focused body. 1 caller (only invoked via the dispatcher).
executeActorType2Effect         = 0x800F4E74;
// (actor, intensity f32) — type-3 effect executor, dispatched from dispatchEffectByActorType. 1 caller. Same +0x198 NPC-context prologue as type-1/2.
executeActorType3Effect         = 0x800F538C;

executeActorType4Effect         = 0x800F8398;
// (actor, a1 f32, a2 f32, a3 f32) — type-1-guarded setter. Returns 0 silently if actor+0x112 != 1. On match: writes (a1 × MEM_W[0x800A8F50]) to actor+0x68, (a2) to actor+0x84, (a3) to actor+0xD4. All offsets are relative to actor+0x8 base. Velocity-style 3-component setter for type-1 actors.
setType1ActorVelocity           = 0x800F97B4;
// (actor, value f32) — type-1-guarded swap of actor+0x40 float. If actor+0x112 != 1: returns 0. Else: reads old MEM_W[actor+0x40] into f0 (return), writes new value (a1) to actor+0x40, returns the old value.
setType1ActorField40            = 0x800F97F4;
// (actor, parent_ref u32, init_f f32, init_f2 f32) — converts an actor into a type-2 actor: sets actor+0x112 = 2, then reads actor+0x198 (cue id), looks up via getNpcContextField8 to get NPC context, copies its position (ctx+0xC/+0x10/+0x14) into actor+0x8/+0xC/+0x10. Zero-initializes a 0x25-byte block at actor+0x18..+0x3D (the type-2 extended state — float fields at +0x1C/+0x20/+0x24/+0x28/+0x2C all set to 0). Writes a3 (init_f2) → actor+0x14, a2 (init_f) → actor+0x18, actor+0x1C/+0x20/+0x24, a1 (parent_ref) → actor+0x38, zero bytes at actor+0x3C and +0x3D. Returns 1.
initActorAsType2                = 0x800F981C;
// (actor, a1 f32, a2 f32, a3 f32) — sister to setType1ActorVelocity for type 2. Returns 0 if actor+0x112 != 2. On match: writes the 3 floats into actor+0x8+0xZZ offsets (similar layout to type-1 variant; sister handler for type-2 actors).
setType2ActorVelocity           = 0x800F98CC;
// (actor) -> f0 — 3 callers, type-polymorphic getter returning the actor's "current value" float based on actor+0x112: type 1 → MEM_W[actor+0x50] (the type-1 ramp current from updateType1AnimRamp), type 4 → MEM_W[actor+0x28] (type-4 storage offset). Returns 0.0 for any other type. Used by external readers that don't care about which type-specific storage the actor uses.
getActorCurrentValueByType      = 0x800F9BEC;
// (actor, a1 f32, a2 f32, a3 f32, [sp+0x10]=p4 f32, [sp+0x14]=p5 f32) -> v0 — type-3-guarded setter. Returns 0 if actor+0x112 != 3. On match (a0+8 base): writes 0 to base+0x18, a1 → base+0x24 (actor+0x2C), a2 → base+0x28 (actor+0x30), a3 → base+0x1C (actor+0x24), p4 → base+0x20 (actor+0x28), p5 → base+0x2C (actor+0x34). Returns 1. The 5-float type-3 extended-state setter (block layout at actor+0x24..+0x34).
setType3ActorParams             = 0x800F99C8;
// (actor, src, target f32, duration f32, [sp+0x40]=initFloat f32, ...) — 15 callers, comprehensive type-1 initializer. Sets actor+0x112 = 1, stores src ptr at actor+0x2C, copies src+0x1C → actor+0x8 (id field), src+0x20 → actor+0xC. Computes actor+0x10 = src+0x20 + src+0x1C * 12 (stride-12 array index). Zeroes the ramp channels at actor+0x4C/0x50/0x54/0x60 etc. Initializes ramp constants: actor+0x24 = MEM_W[0x800A8F2C] (1.0 float?), actor+0x48 = actor+0x50 = target (a2), actor+0x2C = target / src+0x24 (rate). Clamps actor+0x30 = duration × rate against MEM_W[0x800A8F2C]. If src+0x28 ptr is non-null, reads src+0x2C[0] → actor+0x40 (color/scale lookup); else uses default MEM_W[0x800A8F30]. Companion to initActorAsType2.
initActorAsType1                = 0x800F947C;
// (actor, src, target f32, duration f32, [sp+0x40]=initFloat f32, ...) — 1 caller. Nearly-identical variant of initActorAsType1 (same field layout, same ramp/duration math) but after the base init, additionally calls getNpcContextField8(actor+0x198) to fetch NPC ctx, then func_800662A0(actor+0x8, src+0x10, npcCtx, actor+0xC, actor+0x30) to apply an NPC-anchored transform/position setup. Use the variant when the actor needs world-space positioning from the NPC anchor; use the bare initActorAsType1 when caller will handle positioning itself.
initActorAsType1WithAnchor      = 0x800F9674;
// (actor, parent_ref u32, init_f f32, init_f2 f32) — 1 caller, sister of initActorAsType2. Sets actor+0x112 = 3, reads actor+0x198 → getNpcContextField8 → NPC ctx, copies ctx+0xC/+0x10/+0x14 (NPC pos XYZ) into actor+0x8/+0xC/+0x10. Zeroes the 0x25-byte extended state at actor+0x18..+0x3D. Writes init_f2 → actor+0x14, init_f → actor+0x18, parent_ref → actor+0x38, zero bytes at +0x3C/+0x3D. Returns 1. Almost identical to initActorAsType2 except for the type byte value.
initActorAsType3                = 0x800F9918;
// (actor, assetName ptr u32, ...) — 4 callers. Sets actor+0x112 = 4, then if assetName+0x0 is non-null: looks up the named DAT item via getLevelDatItemByName, validates result, branches on the item's byte at +0x2 (== 6, == 7, etc.) into format-specific paths. Sets actor+0x58 (= s2 = actor+0x50+0x8) for further setup. The type-4 actor variant is asset-name-driven (different from types 1-3 which are NPC-anchored). Likely used for spawning level-defined effects/objects by name.
initActorAsType4                = 0x800F5A30;
// () — gates on MEM_BU[0x8010C9F0] (request counter, must be 0 = idle); calls isSpeechBusyOrQueued. If speech is busy/queued: writes MEM_B[0x80130B14] = 2 (response-mode 2), OR's MEM_W[0x8010C9E0] with 0x1 (request-pending bit), increments MEM_B[0x8010C9F0] (counter). Sister of requestSpeechResponseMode1 — same shape, different mode value.
requestSpeechResponseMode2      = 0x800FBAE8;
// () — sister of requestSpeechResponseMode2; identical shape except writes MEM_B[0x80130B14] = 1 (response-mode 1) instead of 2. Same idle-counter gate, same isSpeechBusyOrQueued check, same pending-bit and counter increments.
requestSpeechResponseMode1      = 0x800FBB44;
// (actor) — 2 callers, type-1-guarded short reset. Returns immediately if actor+0x112 != 1. Else writes MEM_W[actor+0xFC] = 0 and MEM_W[actor+0x100] = MEM_W[0x800A8F54] (constant float, likely default "rate" or "alpha"). Pairs with setType1ActorParams (which writes the broader +0x104..+0x10C block) but resets just the +0xFC/+0x100 pair.
resetType1FxFields              = 0x800F9B0C;
// (channel, programPtr=0x80130CC0) — MusyX sequencer command-0x40 handler (decimal 64) dispatched from tickAudioChannel. Pushes one event into the channel's event ring at channel+0xC0 (4-byte entries, max 4 entries gated by counter at channel+0xD0). Each entry: byte+0 = MIDI status (0x80/0x82/0xA0/0xA1/0x83/0x84 selected from 6-entry sub-switch on the program byte's mid nibble), byte+1 = src+0x4, halfword+2 = src+0x0 scaled by a fixed-point quotient. Ring entry counter clamped to <4. Also OR's flag 0x100000 (bit 20) into channel+0x24 if the program byte+0x7 is zero. Companion ring to the other 6 seqCmd handlers (each writes a different ring on the channel).
seqCmdHandler40                 = 0x8008B33C;
// (channel, programPtr) — MusyX cmd-0x41 (decimal 65). Writes to the event ring at channel+0xD2, counter at channel+0xE2.
seqCmdHandler41                 = 0x8008B42C;
// (channel, programPtr) — MusyX cmd-0x42 (decimal 66). Writes to the event ring at channel+0xF6, counter at channel+0x106. (Note: skips the ring at channel+0xE4/+0xF4 which is managed by a different code path — that slot's default status byte is 0x83 per tickAudioChannel init.)
seqCmdHandler42                 = 0x8008B51C;
// (channel, programPtr) — MusyX cmd-0x43 (decimal 67). Writes to channel+0x11A ring, counter at channel+0x12A. (Skips the +0x108/+0x118 ring — that slot's default status is 0x84.)
seqCmdHandler43                 = 0x8008B60C;
// (channel, programPtr) — MusyX cmd-0x44 (decimal 68). Writes to channel+0x12C ring, counter at channel+0x13C. Default initial status byte for this ring is 0x40 (MIDI CC#64 Sustain Pedal per tickAudioChannel init).
seqCmdHandler44                 = 0x8008B6FC;
// (channel, programPtr) — MusyX cmd-0x45 (decimal 69). Writes to channel+0x13E ring, counter at channel+0x14E. Default initial status byte is 0x41 (MIDI CC#65 Portamento).
seqCmdHandler45                 = 0x8008B7EC;
// (channel, programPtr) — MusyX cmd-0x46 (decimal 70). Writes to channel+0x150 ring, counter at channel+0x160. Default initial status byte is 0x5B (MIDI CC#91 Reverb Send Level).
seqCmdHandler46                 = 0x8008B8DC;
// 3 callers, per-frame audio sequencer driver: zeroes 4 frame counters (MEM_W[0x80140448], +0x80140458, +0x8013F03C, +0x80140454). Reads global tempo from MEM_W[0x80142038]; if non-zero, computes samples-per-tick via `(tempo × 0x7C × 0x100) / MEM_W[0x801440C0]` (the tempo divisor), shifts left 3, and stores result to MEM_W[0x8014ACC8] (the per-tick sample budget consumed by tickAudioChannel's +0x40 counter). Then calls tickAllAudioChannels to advance every active channel's sequencer one tick.
tickAudioSequencerFrame         = 0x8008D960;
// (parent, x i16, y i16) — 10 callers. Sign-extends a1/a2 from 16-bit, converts to float, writes the floats to MEM[parent+0xC]+0x18 and +0x1C respectively. Companion to getStructField0xC at 0x800B3D08 (the trivial getter for parent+0xC ptr). Used to set a sub-struct's 2D position fields from integer screen-space coords — likely HUD / UI / 2D-overlay positioning.
setChildPosFromI16Pair          = 0x800B3CD4;
// service-worker tick handler. Loops: recvServiceMessage(workerKey) (blocking) → process result msg s3 via func_800B2630(msg+0x14, msg+0x18, workerKey). Then walks msg+0x14 → +0x8 chain doing sprintf_recomp formatting (uses global format string at MEM_W[0x800A5C40] as fmt arg) into local stack buffers at sp+0x52 and sp+0x84+0x2. Registered at priority 5 by initFormatMessageWorker.
tickFormatMessageWorker         = 0x800B3650;
// (priorityOverride u8) — registers + starts the tickFormatMessageWorker via registerServiceWorker(8 msgSlots, 0x800 stack, priority, &tickFormatMessageWorker). Stores returned worker ID at MEM_W[0x800CC854] and the same value at MEM_W[0x800CC858] (registry handle). Then calls startServiceWorker to launch the thread. Both callers pass priorityOverride = 5.
initFormatMessageWorker         = 0x800B3EB8;
// unregisters the format-message worker thread. Reads stored handle from MEM_W[0x800CC858] and calls unregisterServiceWorker(handle).
shutdownFormatMessageWorker     = 0x800B3F00;
// (parent, member) — disposes a queue member's allocated buffers. If parent+0x1C flag bits 0x6 are set: rs_free(member+0x3C) and func_800C6DBC(member+0x70) (alternate-format buffer). Always: rs_free(member+0x38) and func_800C6DBC(member+0x6C) (primary buffer). Cleanup helper used when removing a member from the format-message queue.
disposeFormatMsgQueueMember     = 0x800B3E5C;
// (scene) — 9 callers, scene cleanup. If scene+0x1CC (NPC slot chain head u16) != 0xFFFF: loops calling getNextSlotNpcTypeId(slot) then destroyNpcSlotByIndex(slot) until type == 0xFFFF, then destroyNpcSlotChain(slot), then resets scene+0x1CC = 0xFFFF. If scene+0x24C (active byte) is non-zero: clears it and calls factor5RemoveListNode(scene+0x208) to unlink the scene from its parent list. Used by mission/objective scenes when shutting down (mission complete, fail, restart).
destroySceneNpcsAndUnlink       = 0x800B4134;
// (config, triggerSpec) — 9 callers, guarded 3D sound trigger. Multiple gates: global mute at MEM_BU[0x80138838], system mute at MEM_BU[0x80138926], per-player level flag at MEM_W[0x80130B40 + config+0xB8 * 4]+0xC bit 0x800000, per-player NPC flag at gPlayerSlotTable[playerIdx]+0x184 bits 0x78. When all gates pass AND triggerSpec+0x8 != 0: calls play3DSoundEvent with (sound=0, pos=config+0x4, alt=config+0x28, maxDist=24.0f, distScale=MEM_W[0x800A57BC], vol=0x7F, ?=0x1B, ?=0x5A). Used as the "play gated 3D effect sound" entry by various weapon/explosion/impact events.
tryPlaySpatialSoundWithGates    = 0x800B3A10;
// (scene) — if scene+0x1C0 (queue handle u32, sentinel -1 = empty) is not -1: calls func_80002BA8(handle) to cancel/release the handle and resets scene+0x1C0 = -1. Companion to enqueueFormatMessage which would have populated +0x1C0 with an active handle. Used when shutting down a scene that has a pending format-message request.
cancelSceneQueueHandle          = 0x800B3F0C;
// (token u32, string ptr) — PRODUCER side of the format-message queue. Allocates a 0x60-byte queue member via rs_malloc, rs_memsets to 0. Fills: member+0x14 = token (a0, scene-correlation id), member+0x58 = MEM_W[0x80139920] (current state global), member+0x5C = MEM_W[0x800CC854] (the worker's registration ID — same one initFormatMessageWorker stored). rs_strcpy's the input string (a1) into member+0x18 (the queue member's string buffer). Then sendServiceMessage(workerKey=MEM_W[0x80139920], handle=MEM_W[0x800CC858], member, 0) to enqueue. The tickFormatMessageWorker thread will pick up the member, process the string, and call disposeFormatMsgQueueMember to free buffers when done.
enqueueFormatMessage            = 0x800B3F24;

// MusyX micro-handlers (funcs_22) — each manipulates one specific channel struct field; likely called from a dispatcher table separate from the tickAudioChannel inline switch
// (channel) — reads MEM_W[channel+0x0], shifts left 8 bits, writes back. Advances/manipulates the channel's program-stream state word.

setChannelKeyOffsetAt6E     = 0x80089F0C;

// Generic binary-search primitive
// (a0=keyArgForCmp, a1=tableBase, a2=count, a3=entryStride, [sp+0x48]=cmpFuncPtr) -> entryPtr | 0. Generic binary search that calls the caller-provided comparator function (via LOOKUP_FUNC) at each midpoint. Returns the matching entry pointer (table + idx*stride) on hit, 0 on miss. Used with cmpU16ByValue and compareHalfwordAtOffset4 (already named) as sorted-table lookups for MusyX subsystem data (likely sample indices or program offsets). Returns 0 when a2 (count) is 0.
binarySearchWithComparator = 0x80085C0C;

// (saveSlotIdx u8) — initializes gMissionState bytes 0..255 to 3 (default lives), then reads save-slot record from 20-byte-stride table at 0x8013A5D4; copies pilot name via rs_strcpy to gGameSettings+0x7, copies status byte at slot+0x4 to gMissionState+0x1, applies packed config bits from slot+0xC into gGameSettings+0xC. Called from menuControllerInput (save-select / new-game flow).
applySaveSlotToMissionState      = 0x8006DB2C;
// (meshAsset) — if meshAsset+0xC has "release pending" bit 0x10 set, copies 7 words of metadata (+0x3C, +0x44, +0x48..+0x58) into next slot of the 28-byte-stride per-frame release queue at 0x80112B30 (count u16 at 0x80113230, capacity 64). On success clears flag bits 3+4 of meshAsset+0xC. On overflow (count >= 64) returns silently leaving the pending bit set, so the caller retries next frame — the 64 ceiling is a per-frame work budget, not a total-mesh limit. Called from releaseMeshAsset and 3 sister funcs.
enqueueMeshForDeferredRelease    = 0x80008350;
// (meshAsset) — per-frame deferred-ref resolver: requires meshAsset+0xC bits 0x10 AND 0x60 set, then clears bits 0x60 (keeps 0x10). Picks source-list pair based on meshAsset+0x2C bit 31 — either (+0x44/+0x4C) or (+0x48/+0x50). Walks both linked lists; for each ~64-entry batch chunk at meshAsset+0x58 (chained via +0x4), per entry: reads type byte at +0x8>>24, then writes resolved source ptr into entry+0x4 (type 0 → a2 list, type 1 → a1 list). Called per-frame from submitGfxFrame over the active-mesh array at 0x8010E090 (count u16 at 0x8010DFD4), immediately after drainMeshReleaseQueue.
commitMeshDrawBatchRefs          = 0x80008550;
// per-submitGfxFrame: walks the deferred-release queue at 0x80112B30 (count at 0x80113230, capacity 64 × 28 bytes); for each entry traverses its attached resource list and frees each via the chain rooted at MEM[0x801143B0]. Resets count to 0 at end, so each frame starts with an empty queue. Companion to enqueueMeshForDeferredRelease.
drainMeshReleaseQueue            = 0x8000815C;
// takes (texIdx u16); walks doubly-linked list at materialListHead[texIdx] (head ptr table at 0x80128F00, stride 4). For each node: calls func_800079A4 (per-node cleanup), unlinks via next/prev (offsets 0x0/0x4), splices into free-list at 0x80128EF8. Called from freeTextureDataSlot.
unlinkTextureMaterialList        = 0x80021F78;
// per-channel sequencer tick: decrements wait counter at +0x40, processes one MIDI-like command from program stream, dispatches to one of 7 sub-handlers (8008B33C..8008B8DC). Channel struct size 0x17C.

// per-frame video advance — complement of initVideoBootWrapper. Waits frame-ready msg, advances buffer arbiter via bufferArbiterMarkSlotReady + bufferArbiterProducerMark, calls clearPostSwapPendingFlags + swapBufferWithViMode + buildAndRegisterDefaultMaterial (which also writes the active draw fb to MEM_W[0x8011A84C]). Caller chain: runVideoFrameTick → advanceVideoFrame. The fb the SET_COLOR_IMAGE emitter at 0x80009124 reads comes from buildAndRegisterDefaultMaterial's write here.
advanceVideoFrame    = 0x8001818C;
// per-frame video dispatch wrapper. Reads 6+ struct args (rect/sprite/material/display-config), repackages them onto the stack for advanceVideoFrame's signature, calls advanceVideoFrame, then if return value & 0xFF is non-zero AND MEM_BU[0x8010D211] is set AND s2+0x4 is non-zero: reads display-config fields and calls reapplyDisplayMode. 4 callers (funcs_0 thread3_video_handle chain, funcs_8 ×2, funcs_27, funcs_34) — shared "advance one frame, apply pending mode change if needed" primitive.

// osViSetMode + osViSwapBuffer (small wrapper called from per-screen video reconfig)

// blocking osRecvMesg on the VI/buffer-arbiter queue at 0x80128CF0, with a visible "waiting" byte flag at MEM_B[0x80037808] set during the wait. After recv, clears the flag and osYieldThread. 3 callers: advanceVideoFrame, bufferArbiterProducerScanWait, initVideoSubsystem.
waitOnVideoQueue     = 0x8001BE80;

// Batch 29: per-frame rendering threads (created by initVideoSubsystem)

// RDP done handler thread; osSetEventMesg(OS_EVENT_DP=9, q), waits, notifies

// Batch 30: framebuffer arbiter state-machine (6 states: FREE/ALLOC/READY/CONSUMED/PRESENTED/DISPLAYED)
// 0→1: scans for FREE slot, marks ALLOC
bufferArbiterAllocSlot        = 0x8001BF20;
// 1→2: scans for ALLOC slot, marks READY
bufferArbiterMarkSlotReady    = 0x8001BEC8;
// 2→3 (mark CONSUMED) and X→4 (post-osViSwapBuffer mark PRESENTED); the SP task producer
bufferArbiterProducerMark     = 0x8001BCE4;
// producer-side wait: calls bufferArbiterMarkSlotReady + bufferArbiterProducerMark, scans 4-slot state table at 0x80128EAA looking for any slot in state 3 (CONSUMED) or 4 (PRESENTED); osRecvMesg on consumer-ack queue, osYield, repeat (per src/main/rt64_render_context.cpp comment "scan loop spinning waiting for state 3 or 4")
bufferArbiterProducerScanWait = 0x8000BF60;

// Batch 31: per-frame gfx orchestrator (THE key function — each screen calls this to present)
// per-frame: alloc → markReady → SP task → 3× producerMark + osViSwapBuffer; called from ~14 screen handlers (cinematic/menu/mission overlays)
submitGfxFrame         = 0x8000C07C;
// returns byte at 0x80138D2C XOR 1 (binary inactive-fb index for double-buffer)
getInactiveBufferIndex = 0x80019BE4;

// osGetTime() → return low 32 bits (drops high)

// post-osViSwapBuffer: osDpSetStatus + osWritebackDCacheAll + osSendMesg
postSwapRdpReset      = 0x8001C12C;
// tiny osYieldThread wrapper (called by submitGfxFrame after SP task kick)
yieldFromSpThread     = 0x8001A018;
// tiny osYieldThread wrapper (called twice mid-submitGfxFrame)

markLowLodCellsInGrid  = 0x80043AEC;

processMeshdef1ForLod  = 0x80014DE0;

// Menu-entry / overlay helpers (children of menuOverlayInit per call-graph analysis).

Wraps the hangar selection flow when entered from a menu overlay.
runHangarMenuFromOverlay = 0x800C6854;

// NPC destruction handlers — share signature pattern: spawnDebris1Npc + spawnNpcType0xAFromTemplate

endMissionCleanup = 0x800FB9E4;

// Cinematic dispatch from main loop. Scans 16-byte slot status array (a0[0..15])

dispatchCinematicFromMainLoop = 0x800AE878;

// Initialize all 12 player slots. Iterates gPlayerSlotTable[0..11] and for
// each slot pointer: zeros fields +0x20, +0x24, +0x28, +0x2C, +0x30, +0xEC,
// +0xF0, +0xF4, +0xFC; inner-loops 20 entries at +0x34 (4-byte stride) clearing
// +0x84 byte. Stamps slot[+0xF8] with ASCII identifier 'A'..'L' (0x41 + idx).
// Finally sets state byte at 0x80139960=1, halfwords at +0x962/+0x964 = 0x140.
// Called from mainGameLoop at game/level start.
// (90L) main-loop child
initAllPlayerSlots = 0x80063B58;

// First-order Taylor expansion of a path/position: out = base - neg_velocity * t.
// Equivalently P(t) ≈ P(0) + P'(0)*t with delta stored as -P'(0) ("approach delta"
// pointing from target back to base). Per-frame path integrator for predefined enemy
// flight paths and sound-position prediction. Pure vec3 math, no globals.
// Args: (struct{vec3 base@0; vec3 negVel@C}* a0, ?, vec3* out, float t).
// 7 callers across npcShuttleUpdate, npcTrainCarUpdate, npcWordDevastatorUpdate,
// playerAtStUpdate, tryPlaySpatialSoundWithGates (3x for spatial sound offset).
vec3ExtrapolateFirstOrder = 0x80077784;

// Second-order Taylor integrator with circular-motion cross-product correction.
// Args: (?, npc_a1, pathState_a2, timeScalars_a3) where:
//   pathState (a2): vec3 P @ +0x00, vec3 vel @ +0x0C, vec3 ang_vel @ +0x18
//   timeScalars (a3): t²/2 (or weight) @ +0x00, t @ +0x04, more scalars @ +0x10
// Math: result = P + vel·t + cross(ang_vel, vel) · (t²/2)
//   (where cross(ω, v) is the centripetal-acceleration approximation for
//   uniform circular motion). Per-axis formula matches:
//     out.x = P.x + vel.x*s1[+4] + (ang_vel.x*vel.z - ang_vel.y*vel.y) * s1[+0]
// Then calls wrapAngleToCyclicRange (orientation wrap), reads global config at
// *(0x80136E08)+0x10, calls func_8006B844 (banking/orientation), and finally
// setVec3At0x24(npc+0x3C, x, y, z) to overwrite the NPC transform matrix
// with identity-rotation + new translation. 19 callers — the primary path-step
// driver for npcAtAt/ShuttleUpdate/TrainCar/GroundTransport/GroundTroop/etc.
advanceNpcOnCurvedPath = 0x8006BEC8;

// Companion to advanceNpcOnCurvedPath (only caller, 1099 lines).
// Computes 4 banking-corrected world-space points at NPC+0x178..+0x198 from
// pathState (vel, ang_vel) and writes them after wrapping with
// wrapAngleToCyclicRange.
// Per-corner formula: corner = anchor + base ± (ang_vel × vel) * scale
// where sign of (ω×v) flips based on sign(ang_vel.y) (rotation direction).
// The 4 corners are likely wing-tip / banking sample points used for
// shadow casting, terrain collision, or follow-on transform composition.
// f8 scaling factor = NPC[+0x8], f6 = NPC[+0xC] (per-craft tuning).
applyCentripetalBankingToNpcCorners = 0x8006B844;

// Terrain-grid sampler — converts world (x,z) to grid cell index using level
// grid block at *(0x80136DC0) (+0x20=origin x, +0x2C=origin y, +0x10=stride,
// inverse-cell-size constants at 0x8003C838..0x8003C840), then walks the
// grid to sample terrain height / orientation. Calls only func_80068A90
// (1-of-1 helper, likely cell-data fetch) and wrapAngleToCyclicRange.
// Args: (vec3* a0_pos, vec3* a1, vec3* a2, struct* a3_out) — writes terrain
// height + orientation pair to a3_out+0x18/+0x1C. Returns 1 on success.
// 10 callers — ground walkers (npcAtAt/AtSt/AtPt), advanceNpcOnCurvedPath,
// and a few other path-following helpers (npcAtPt-related funcs).
sampleTerrainAtNpcPosition = 0x80068D5C;

// Bicubic terrain-height interpolation from an HMP tile heightmap.
// 4x4 nested loop: outer a2 iterates 0..3 (rows), inner a1 iterates 0..3
// (cols). Reads byte heights at cell+0x5 (6-byte stride matches rerogue
// HMP tile layout: 5 metadata bytes + 1 height byte per cell). Computes
// finite-difference gradients between neighbor cells and accumulates a
// smooth bicubic interpolation. No function calls — pure math.
// Args: (f12 fract_x, f14 fract_z, a2 cell_offset, a3 ?, sp+0x40 weight_x,
//        sp+0x44 weight_z, sp+0x48 tile_base_ptr). Returns interpolated
// height in f0. Bounds-checked against constants at 0x80037628..0x80037638
// (origin / scale / half_cell / inv_scale).
sampleHmpTileHeightBicubic = 0x80068A90;

// DAT subtype dispatcher (subtypes 0x01..0x05) — invoked via vtable (no direct callers in .c).
// 5-way switch on (a1-1) where a1 = DAT subtype byte:
//   case 0 (0x01): DAT_TYPE_0_PLAYER_START_POSITION → L_800477F4
//   case 1 (0x02): DAT_TYPE_0_RESTART_POSITION → L_800479A0
//   case 2 (0x03): DAT_TYPE_3_03 spline → L_800479DC (calls getCraftRecordByIdx)
//   case 3 (0x04): DAT_TYPE_6_HIGH_LOD_BOX → L_80047AA0
//   case 4 (0x05): DAT_TYPE_6_LOW_LOD_BOX → L_80047A88
// Each case calls parseDatItemCommon + subtype-specific setup.
parseDatItemSubtypes1To5 = 0x800477A0;

// Per-craft record table accessor — returns pointer to a 672-byte (0x2A0) stride record at
// gCraftRecordTable+4 + idx*0x2A0 (base at 0x80137DBC; records start at +4). Math:
//   v2 = (idx*5)*4 = 20*idx; v2 += idx → 21*idx; v2 <<= 5 → 672*idx; return base+v2+4.
// 9 callers: per-craft DAT/object setup including npcWordDevastatorUpdate, getAvailableShipsForLevel,
// npcPowerUpUpdate, executeActorType4Effect, and parseDatItemSubtypes1To5 case 2 (spline).
getCraftRecordByIdx = 0x8006C8F0;
// Note: func_8006C918 is a verbatim duplicate (same math, same table). Skipped per
// no-address-encoded-name rule — there's no semantic distinction between the two.

// Active-craft spatial-partition entry — converts position to grid cell index, clamps to
// grid bounds (width at *0x801375F0, height at *0x801375EC), iterates the active-cell table
// at *0x801375D0 to clear stale entries against count at *0x801375D4. Reads inv-cell-size
// constants at 0x8003A7B8/0x8003A7BC (sector grid, distinct from the level LOD grid at
// 0x80136DC0). 2 callers: lookupActivePlayerCraftGridCell + cinematicLoopBody (camera POV
// during cutscenes).
updateActiveGridCellState = 0x80047368;

// Per-frame from runInMissionFrame: fetches the default craft's record (or fallback
// constant 0x801375F8 if game-state bit 3 at 0x8010CA1C is set), then calls
// updateActiveGridCellState on it. Used to track where the player POV is in the world's
// sector grid for spatial-partition lookups (nearby NPCs, terrain, audio gates).
lookupActivePlayerCraftGridCell = 0x80047E0C;

// MusyX active flag — single byte gating most audio API entry points
// (factor5RemoveListNode, unlinkMusyXListNode, applyMusicOrSfxVolume,
// playSpeechClip, etc.). Set during MusyX init, cleared on shutdown.
gMusyXActiveFlag = 0x800A0F50; // type:u8

// One-line getter for gMusyXActiveFlag — returns the MusyX-active byte
// in v0 with no other side effects. Likely the public API entry that
// other modules call to test if audio is up.
isMusyXActive = 0x8008ED5C;

// AT-AT / Walker leg articulation pipeline (traced 2026-05-12).
// Star Wars walker NPCs (AT-AT, World Devastator, etc.) use a shared leg-stride
// animation primitive driven by a phase counter at npc+0x6D.

// AT-AT-specific leg-stride update. Called from npcAtAtUpdate ONCE per frame
// when a float condition is met (after incrementing npc+0x6D step counter).
// Calls lookupHmtTextureByName("0x800A6DCC", npc.assetData, ..., stride_buf)
// to fetch the next stride keyframe, then computeWalkerLimbStrideTargets to
// derive 3 vec3 outputs (likely left foot, right foot, body offset). Scales
// each by global at *(0x800A6EC8).
updateAtAtLegArticulation = 0x800CD558;

// Shared walker leg-stride target computer (2 callers: AT-AT and World
// Devastator). Args: (npcIdx u16, &posBuf out, &outBuf1 out, &outBuf2 out,
// sp+0xA8=scale_f). Calls getNpcContextField8 + mat34CrossRowsAndCopyPos to
// build the walker body's normal-frame, then transformLimbStrideAnchorsLocal
// to fetch 2 foot-anchor positions in body-local space, then scales by global
// at *(0x8003D1F8). Output: 3 vec3 buffers used as leg-IK targets.
computeWalkerLimbStrideTargets = 0x8007602C;

// Transform 2 vec3 stride anchors (npc parent's +0x5C and +0x68 fields) into
// body-local space via transformVec3ByMat34. Writes to sp+0x70 and sp+0x80
// of the caller. 5 callers across walker / large-NPC update functions.
// Per the shared pipeline, these 2 vec3s are the start-of-stride and
// end-of-stride foot-target positions for one limb cycle.
transformLimbStrideAnchorsLocal = 0x80074B74;

// Main AT-AT body transform updater (1473 lines, 51 named calls including
// buildRotationMatrixSlot0/1, mat34CrossRowsAndCopyPos, factor5RemoveListNode,
// getObjectMeshTransformOrLocal, isPendingChildNpcActive). Called twice from
// npcAtAtUpdate per frame on alternative branches — the heavy per-frame body
// pose update applying orientation, mesh transforms, and child-segment
// management. Args: (npc s1, f22=animation_scale_float).
tickAtAtBodyTransform = 0x800CC2AC;

// Per-frame round-robin sub-feature processor. Each call builds a 3-char
// asset name "sf" + (npc[+0x6E] + '1') on the stack and looks it up via
// lookupHmtTextureByName, then findActiveNpcInSlotChain on the result.
// Counter byte at +0x6E advances frame-by-frame to cycle through child
// segments (likely 4 AT-AT legs as "sf0" through "sf3", or similar
// numbered sub-meshes). Single caller: npcAtAtUpdate.
processAtAtSubFeatureSlot = 0x800CCB1C;

// AT-AT footstep/weapon audio + state update. Reads NPC weapon fields
// at +0x7C, +0x78; calls func_80082EA0 + func_80082CFC; triggers
// play3DSoundEvent; updates NPC state fields at +0xE7 (byte), +0xF0/+0xF4
// (floats, likely cooldown timer), +0x90 (byte). Called BEFORE the body
// transform pass each AT-AT update — likely the footstep thud or weapon
// fire sound trigger synchronized to the +0x6D phase counter.
tickAtAtAudioAndWeaponState = 0x800CC9B8;

// Model animation runtime slot table (32 slots × 20-byte stride at 0x80139AA0).
// Each slot stores: +0x0 active byte, +0x4 entry count, +0x8 secondary table ptr.
// Used by parseModelAnimType2Entry (write) and tickAtAtAudioAndWeaponState et al (read/clear).
// Model-animation slot table — 32 × 20-byte slots. Indexed 1..32 via (slot_id-1)*20.
// Per-slot layout (derived from allocateModelAnimSlot writes + clearModelAnimChannelEntry reads):
//   +0x00: void* fieldA           // caller-supplied (a0 in allocate)
//   +0x04: void* fieldB           // caller-supplied (a2 in allocate)
//   +0x08: void* entryCountSrc    // ptr to count source (a1 in allocate)
//   +0x0C: void* secondaryTable   // rs_malloc'd to *entryCountSrc * 16 bytes
//   +0x10: u8   active            // (cleared on release, set on allocate)
//   +0x11..+0x13: padding
// MIPS access: `at = 0x80140000 + (slot-1)*20`, fields via `at + (-0x5670 + field_offset)`.
gActiveModelAnimSlots = 0x8013A990; // size:0x280 (32 × 20B)

// Mark one entry inactive in a model-animation slot's secondary table.
// Args: a0 = packed (slot_id<<16 | entry_idx). Bounds-checks slot_id ∈ 1..32,
// validates slot is active, then clears byte at slot.secondaryTable[entry_idx*16 + 8].
// 4+ callers across model-anim cleanup paths.
clearModelAnimChannelEntry = 0x80082EA0;

// Release all entries in a model-animation slot. Args: a0 = slot_id (1..32).
// Iterates slot.entryCount entries; for each, calls rs_free(entry[+0xC]) on the
// per-entry data ptr. Then calls rs_free on the secondary table itself.
// 4+ callers — animation-slot cleanup on NPC destruction / scene change.
releaseModelAnimSlot = 0x80082CFC;

// Binary-search keyframe lookup within a model-anim channel entry.
// Args: a0 = slot_id (u8), a1 = entry_idx, a2 = phase float as int.
// Reads slot.secondaryTable[entry_idx*16].keyframeArrayPtr at +0x8, then
// binary-searches for the keyframe whose halfword key brackets the input
// phase. Result placed in caller-supplied output buffer.
findModelAnimKeyframeByPhase = 0x800826A0;

// Seek a model-animation channel to a specific time. Args: a0 packed
// (slot_id<<16 | entry_idx), a1 = time float in seconds.
// Reads entry.cycleDuration at secondaryTable[entry_idx*16+0x4], clamps
// time to [0, duration], normalizes to phase via time/duration, then
// calls findModelAnimKeyframeByPhase to advance the keyframe pointer.
seekModelAnimChannelToTime = 0x80082F18;

// Allocate and initialize a model-animation slot. Args:
//   a0 = void* fieldA (caller pointer, stored at slot+0x00)
//   a1 = void* entryCountSrc (ptr to u32 count; *a1 used to size malloc)
//   a2 = void* fieldB (stored at slot+0x04)
// Scans the 32-slot table for the first inactive slot; on found:
//   slot[+0x00] = a0
//   slot[+0x04] = a2
//   slot[+0x08] = a1 (entryCountSrc pointer)
//   slot[+0x0C] = rs_malloc(*a1 * 16)  // secondary table
//   (active flag at +0x10 set elsewhere in function)
// Returns 1-indexed slot_id on success.
allocateModelAnimSlot = 0x800828D0;

// Reset the entire model-anim slot table — zeros all 5 fields of all 32 slots
// (+0x00, +0x04, +0x08, +0x0C all set to 0; +0x10 active byte cleared).
// Returns 1. Called at scene/game init.
resetModelAnimSlotTable = 0x80082870;

// Inverse of clearModelAnimChannelEntry — sets bit 0x1 of the entry's
// active byte at slot.secondaryTable[entry_idx*16+0x8].
// Args: a0 = slot_id (u8), a1 = entry_idx. Validates slot_id ∈ 1..32 and
// slot is active. Returns packed ((slot_id<<16) | entry_idx) handle.
markModelAnimChannelEntryActive = 0x80082E18;

// Broadcast a cycle-duration float to ALL entries of a model-anim slot.
// Args: a0 = slot_id (u8), a1 = duration float (as int via mtc1).
// For each entry in slot.secondaryTable[0..count-1], writes f0 to entry+0x4
// (the cycle-duration field used by seekModelAnimChannelToTime to normalize).
// 9 callers — all walker NPC updates (AT-AT, AT-PT, AT-ST, plus cinematic).
// Likely per-frame animation-speed sync: faster walker = shorter cycle.
setModelAnimSlotCycleDuration = 0x80083258;

// Set cycle duration on a SINGLE entry of a model-anim slot.
// Args: a0 packed (slot_id<<16 | entry_idx), a1 = duration float (as int via mtc1).
// Validates slot ∈ 1..32 + active, then writes f0 to
// slot.secondaryTable[entry_idx*16+0x4]. Single-entry version of
// setModelAnimSlotCycleDuration.
setModelAnimChannelEntryDuration = 0x80083154;

// Read cycle duration from a SINGLE entry of a model-anim slot.
// Args: a0 packed (slot_id<<16 | entry_idx). Returns f0 = duration if
// slot active, else fallback constant from *(0x8003DB1C) (likely 0.0).
// Mirror getter of setModelAnimChannelEntryDuration.
getModelAnimChannelEntryDuration = 0x800831D0;

// Slot-broadcast version of seekModelAnimChannelToTime: applies the same
// time-clamp + phase-normalize + keyframe seek to ALL entries in a slot.
// Args: a0 = slot_id (u8), a1 = time float. Iterates secondaryTable[0..count-1]
// and calls findModelAnimKeyframeByPhase for each. Used when a walker
// advances all leg/segment channels in lockstep.
seekAllModelAnimChannelsInSlotToTime = 0x80083008;

// Batch 34: post-osViSwapBuffer DP-completion handshake (2026-05-11)
// submitGfxFrame calls setPostSwapPendingFlags right after osViSwapBuffer.
// dpInterruptHandlerThread is *supposed* to clear them when RDP completes
// after the swap (signalling "frame fully displayed"). Code elsewhere uses
// waitForPostSwapAck to block until that signal. Under HLE this never fires
// without the toml gate-NOP patch at 0x8001C3C4 (see project memory).
// sets both flag bytes at 0x8011A8C8/C9 to 1 (called from submitGfxFrame after osViSwapBuffer)
setPostSwapPendingFlags   = 0x8001C244;
// polls flag at 0x8011A8C9 in a osRecvMesg/yield loop until cleared
waitForPostSwapAck        = 0x8001C260;
// clears both flag bytes at 0x8011A8C8/C9 (called by advanceVideoFrame, initVideoBootWrapper)
clearPostSwapPendingFlags = 0x8001C2F8;

// Batch 35: audio-slot validity check + busy-wait pattern (2026-05-11)
// Pattern: a slot table at *(0x801487F8), 0x88 bytes per entry. The first
// byte of each entry is the "active" flag. waitForAnyAudioSlot busy-waits
// in cinematic/menu init for any slot to become active — under HLE with
// stubbed MusyX audio it never does. Three NOPs in rogue_squadron.toml
// break the resulting infinite spin.
// returns 1 if MEM_BU(*(0x801487F8) + i*0x88, 0) != 0 else 0
isAudioSlotActive   = 0x800920FC;
// loops i=0..MEM_BU(0x80141AD0), OR-aggregates isAudioSlotActive(i)
anyAudioSlotActive  = 0x8008E57C;
// busy-wait: loops calling anyAudioSlotActive until non-zero (NOPed in toml at 0x800668CC)
waitForAnyAudioSlot = 0x800668B0;

// load_hmt_and_hob inner helper (count, material_entry*, texture_entry*) per notes line 256
parseHmtMaterials  = 0x80022A00;

// Texture-material cache (512-node pool @0x80128EF4, hash table @0x80128F00, free-list @0x80128EF8).
// Roles confirmed by the matpool-corruption guards in rogue_squadron.toml (their probe strings name them).
// boot: rs_malloc(0x3000)=512x0x18-byte nodes -> pool base, then buildMaterialFreeList. (toml: "createMaterialPool")
createMaterialPool        = 0x80021F38;
// zero the hash table, re-link the 512-node pool into a doubly-linked free-list (head @0x80128EF8). (toml: "buildFreeList")
buildMaterialFreeList     = 0x80021E94;
// byte-identical to buildMaterialFreeList but on the subsystem-reset path (caller func_80017B58); rebuilds the pool
resetMaterialPool         = 0x80021DF4;
// look up a material by id in the hash bucket; on miss -> allocOrEvictMaterialNode + emitMaterialRenderStateDL. (toml: "findOrCreate"; has corruption guards)
findOrCreateMaterial      = 0x800225F8;
// pull a free node off the free-list; when empty, evict the smallest/oldest entry. (toml: "allocOrEvict"; has bounds guard)
allocOrEvictMaterialNode  = 0x8002236C;
// build the material's render-state GBI chain (SETOTHERMODE_H/L, G_TEXTURE tile-scale, combine/pipe from table 0x80037860[flags*8], tile-size); calls loadTextureTile x2 gated by desc flags &0x1C00, chains DL buffers. Called by findOrCreateMaterial
emitMaterialRenderStateDL = 0x80021A1C;
// walk hash bucket for id, match node, zero its expiry/age counter (+0x8) so it survives the next tickTextureMaterialExpiry sweep (the &0x80 gate at 0x801256F8 not fully confirmed)
resetMaterialExpiryById   = 0x80022164;

// Batch 37: per-frame screen primitives + post-cinematic save-data load (2026-05-11)
// Same primitive sequence appears in every "screen" handler in the game —
// boot/scene init (func_8006D900) and controller config (func_800B47C4) both
// call this exact order: poll input → frame-start reset → wait prev frame
// done → render setup → audio submit → submitGfxFrame. Confirmed via
// sister-repo docs/controller_stuff/func_800B47C4.c.
// 4-iter loop over controllers, copies pad data into output buffer at MEM_W(0x80110748)+i*6
pollControllerInputs = 0x80002FF4;
// calls waitForPostSwapAck then func_80001880 (clears flag at 0x8010FEFC)
waitForPrevFrameDone = 0x8000A86C;
// resets per-frame counters at 0x8011A90A/0C/0E/10, calls heapWalker, slot dispatch
frameStartReset      = 0x8000A6CC;
// clears word at 0x8010FEFC to 0 (used by waitForPrevFrameDone)
clearFrameReadyFlag  = 0x80001880;
// zeros 12 floats in struct (offsets 0x00-0x2C) — looks like 4x vec3 or 3x vec4 setup
clearVec4QuadStruct  = 0x8001CA50;

// Post-cinematic save-data loader (called from func_8003DFA0 = scene-init).
// Runs 32 frames of normal game loop (keeps screen rendered while loading),
// then clears gSaveDataBody (0x8013A5C0), loads "save" manifest entry,
// decompresses asset, initSaveData. This is where the game returns from
// cinematic into the menu — AV inside this function (offset +0x22C host
// bytes) blocks the menu from appearing. See
// project_osviblack_rendering_unblocked_2026_05_11.md
// 32-iter frame loop + load+init save data; AVs ~iter 1023 (post-cinematic)
runIdleFramesAndLoadSaveData         = 0x8006D900;

// Cinematic slot interpolation (called by cinematicLoopBody iter).
// Walks a slot table at offset 0x13D8 within a struct, stride 76 bytes per
// entry, calling cinematicSlotUpdate on each. Used during cinematic to
// advance per-slot state machines (object position, fade, particle).
// per-slot cutscene math: reads gCutsceneInfo[0x44] (frame counter), interpolates value/a3, multiplies by a2
cinematicSlotUpdate     = 0x800A6904;
// iterates over slot table (idx*76+0x13D8), calls cinematicSlotUpdate on each
cinematicSlotDispatcher = 0x800A76B8;

// Batch 38: per-frame camera + pre-load game-state reset + HUD/asset helpers (2026-05-11)
// setupCameraMatrices is the 5th call in each game-screen handler's per-frame
// primitive sequence (after pollControllerInputs / frameStartReset etc).
// Confirmed via guPerspective call inside the function body.
// takes camera/viewport struct, computes aspect ratio, calls guPerspective → guScale → guMtxCatL (result at MEM_W(0x8011DC5C)). Also calls emitFrameRdpInitDl right after the matrix setup to emit the per-frame RDP-state DL prologue.
setupCameraMatrices = 0x80016C44;
// 2374-line inline DL-builder for the per-frame RDP setup sequence — emits SETOTHERMODE_L (0xB9), SETOTHERMODE_H (0xBA), SET_COLOR_IMAGE (0xFF), and many more GBI bytes directly via MEM_W writes into the active DL buffer (cursor at MEM_W[0x8011DC58]). Reads the active draw fb from MEM_W[0x8011A84C] (written by buildAndRegisterDefaultMaterial) and embeds it into the SET_COLOR_IMAGE bytes. Only callee is findAndUnlinkSmallestEntry (DL buffer expansion). Sole caller: setupCameraMatrices — runs once per camera/viewport setup. This is THE function that bakes the "current draw fb" into the recompile's DL stream, so any attribution/logo fb-routing question goes through here.
emitFrameRdpInitDl  = 0x80008D20;

// Scene-graph traversal subsystem (named 2026-05-20). Entered from
// setupCameraMatrices, this is the Performer-style scene-graph walker that
// matches Prior 2 in project_engine_architectural_priors.md (sibling at
// offset 0x0, child at offset 0xC). Identified during the matpool-corruption
// freeze investigation — after the matpool fix unblocked the cinematic, a
// second freeze surfaced inside submitSceneNodeRender's heap free-list walk
// (funcs_4.c:3130 — same class of bug as the matpool one, different heap).
// recursive scene-graph walker. Reads node->sibling at +0x0, node->child at +0xC; calls processSceneNode per-node, then recurses on child + iterates to sibling. Single external caller: setupCameraMatrices (line 22653 of funcs_4.c).
traverseSceneGraphRecursive = 0x80015548;
// per-node body of traverseSceneGraphRecursive. Tests node->flags at +0xC for bit 0x4 (early-out) and 0x8 (full path); reads node->object at +0x8, compares object->frameStamp at +0x30 against a global at MEM_W(0x80127894) for frame-already-processed check, registers node into a render-queue table at 0x80116400 (entries: ptr/scratch/owner triples) indexed by counter at 0x80127894. Dispatches between 3 render-state branches that each call submitSceneNodeRender (2 of 3 sites within this function).
processSceneNode            = 0x80014FA0;
// ~2700-line per-node render dispatcher. Stamps frame counter (from MEM_W(0x80127894)) onto node->+0x30, then if node->flags & 0x8 is set, registers pointers to the node's sub-fields (+0x3C/+0x40/+0x44/+0x4C/+0x54/+0x58) in renderer scratch globals at 0x801163FC, 0x801277DC, 0x801274C4, 0x801274AC, 0x801274BC, 0x801274B8. Allocates a render entry via findAndUnlinkSmallestEntry, then splices it into the freelist head at MEM_W(0x801163B0) — the freelist walk-to-tail loop at funcs_4.c:3130 is where the second freeze (after matpool fix) currently hangs when that freelist gets a cycle.
submitSceneNodeRender       = 0x80010014;

resetGameSettingsAndMissionStats     = 0x8006E53C;

// Batch 43: NPC slot list lifecycle (2026-05-11)
// The NPC slot subsystem manages 0x800 entries in a doubly-linked free list.
// Each slot is 8B: +0x00 ctx_ptr, +0x04 next_idx (u16), +0x06 prev_idx (u16).
// initNpcSlotList allocates the 16KB list, allocateNpcSlot pops a free slot,
// unregisterAndFreeNpcSlot returns a slot via context-registration array.
// one-shot: rs_malloc(0x4000) for gNpcSlotList; loops 0..0x7FF building doubly-linked free list; sets gNpcSlotListTail=2 and seeds head/last sentinels
initNpcSlotList          = 0x8003E560;
// pops head from free list at gNpcSlotListTail; if list empty returns -1; if a0!=NULL registers idx in context's array at *(a0+0x1C+count*2), increments count byte at a0+0x1B
allocateNpcSlot          = 0x8003E5F8;
// 127 callers: unregisters slot_idx (a0) from context's (a1) array at +0x1C, decrements count at +0x1B (compact-shift entries), then returns slot to free list (doubly-linked unlink at gNpcSlotList[idx*8])
unregisterAndFreeNpcSlot = 0x8003E7D0;

// Batch 44: matrix multiply + DL buffer allocator (2026-05-11)

// func_80007D74 allocates a 256-byte DL buffer node from a free pool, splices it
// into a doubly-linked active list, returns pointer past the 8-byte header.
// multiplies 3x4 matrix at a0 by 3x4 matrix at a1; result destination = a0 (or somewhere — both matrices are 12 floats at offsets 0x00-0x2C)
mat3x4Multiply            = 0x800191C4;
// 29 callers: (mat3x4, sx, sy, sz as u32-encoded floats) — builds diagonal scale matrix on stack with sx,sy,sz at [0][0],[1][1],[2][2] (translation row=0), then mat3x4Multiply(scaleMat, mat, mat) — applies scale in-place
applyScaleToMat3x4        = 0x8001CB04;
// 96 callers: pops a 256-byte node from MEM_W(0x80116AB0) free list, splices into active doubly-linked list at MEM_W(0x80128EDC); returns ptr+8 (past header). Allocates fresh node via findAndUnlinkSmallestEntry if free list empty.
allocateDisplayListBuffer = 0x80007D74;

// Batch 45: free-list best-fit allocator + texture LUT init (2026-05-11)

// 110 callers: walks N buckets at *(0x80138F00), per-bucket linked via .next (offset 0); finds entry with smallest .size halfword at +0x8; unlinks it from its doubly-linked list (offsets 0x0/0x4). Used as fallback by allocateDisplayListBuffer.
findAndUnlinkSmallestEntry    = 0x8002221C;

lookupHmtTextureByName  = 0x80058360;

// Batch 48: Euler-angle rotation matrix construction (2026-05-11)
// Discovered via func_80059D78 (22 callers) which calls 3 rotation builders in
// sequence to compose XYZ Euler rotation. The "init" variants (CC3C, CD6C, CB64)
// write directly to passed-in dest. The "compose" variants (CB9C, CCCC, CDFC, CB04)
// build on stack then mat3x4Multiply onto dest. Exact axis assignment uncertain —
// they all sin/cos angle*PI/180 with subtly different matrix layouts. Names use
// "Slot" rather than X/Y/Z since the layouts don't match standard rotation
// matrices (Row 1 has -sin AND +sin at different offsets — likely game-specific).
// 22 callers: a0=out 3x3 ptr, a2=angle1, a3=angle2 — calls init→composeY(angle1)→composeZ(angle2), then copies 6 specific elements from stack matrix to a0
composeEulerRotationXYZ    = 0x80059D78;
// (a0=dest mat, a1=angle) — sin/cos-fills dest matrix at offsets 0x00,0x10..0x20; no mat-mul. Companion init for slot 0.
buildRotationMatrixSlot0   = 0x8001CC3C;
// (a0=dest mat, a1=angle) — sin/cos-fills dest matrix offsets differently; no mat-mul. Companion init for slot 1.
buildRotationMatrixSlot1   = 0x8001CD6C;
// (a0=dest mat, a1=angle) — sin/cos-fills dest matrix at offsets 0x00/0x04/0x0C/0x10, zeros translation row; constant 0x800008AC (deg-to-rad). Companion init for slot 2.
buildRotationMatrixSlot2   = 0x8001CE9C;
// (a0=dest mat, a1=angle) — builds rotation on stack with constant 0x884, then mat3x4Multiply onto dest
composeRotationMatrixSlot0 = 0x8001CB9C;
// (a0=dest mat, a1=angle) — same pattern with constant 0x894
composeRotationMatrixSlot1 = 0x8001CCCC;
// (a0=dest mat, a1=angle) — same pattern with constant 0x8A4
composeRotationMatrixSlot2 = 0x8001CDFC;

// More 3D math helpers from the HOB rendering / object orientation system.
// 31 callers: Gram-Schmidt — given struct at a0 with right vec3 (+0xC) and up vec3 (+0x18): normalize right, compute temp=cross(right,up)/normalized, then up=cross(right,temp). Result: orthonormal {right, ?, up}.
